Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The potentials operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 959. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 412 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the potentials decomposition. By minimality, the element a^959 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 412-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
